[EVENT: SYR118603]
Date: 2023-10-01
Country: Syria | Location: Ras al-Ain
Type: Violence against civilians > Abduction/forced disappearance | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: National Police Forces [Rebel group]
Actor2: Civilians (Syria) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Alkhabour; SHAAM; SOHR [Other-Subnational]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 October 2023, National Police Forces detained a QDW fighter as he was heading to Turkey from Ras al-Ain city in rural Al Hasakeh following clashes between regime members and QDW fighters in Al Hasakeh city last month. The victim was detained after attempting to enter Turkey with the help of Ahrar al-Sharqiya-affiliated smugglers via smuggling routes. His whereabouts remain unknown.
